## LIVE-WALKTHROUGH-20261022 — طريقة القبول المعتمدة من المالك

طلب المالك العمل خطوة بخطوة على الموقع المنشور مع التصحيح والأمان وسرعة الاستجابة، وتقليل تكرار الاختبارات والتقارير والتوكن. هذا تعديل لطريقة القبول، لا إذن جديد لنشر أو دفع أو استدعاء مدفوع أو تغيير Production أو نقل ملكية الكاتب.

الأسلوب: حركة حقيقية واحدة → نتيجة مرئية + تحقق خادمي محدود → قياس الاستجابة → أخضر والانتقال، أو عطل واحد وتصحيحه ثم إعادة الحركة المتأثرة فقط. لا إعادة شاملة لرحلات نجحت على نفس النسخة ما لم يتغير اعتمادها. كل الجولة على الموقع الفعلي وبحسابات مخصصة لنا دون بيانات عملاء. لا إنشاء موقع أو بيئة إضافية. نسجل سطرًا واحدًا لكل خطوة: النسخة، النتيجة، دليل الحفظ/المنع، الزمن الفعلي، العطل التالي. حالة البداية لجميع الخطوات أدناه: لم تنفذ في هذه المراجعة.

1. **النسخة:** تثبيت الرابط والـSHA المنشور وتوافق العقود المطلوبة، مع تحديد الكاتب الحالي ومسار نشر التصحيح المعتمد. لا يبدأ قبول نسخة مجهولة أو مختلفة عن الإصلاح.
2. **الحساب:** دخول وخروج وإعادة فتح الموقع بحسابنا؛ العودة للحساب الصحيح ومنع فتح صفحة محمية بعد الخروج.
3. **التجهيز والطلب:** إدخال بيانات النشاط والفرع واختيار الباقة ومراجعة السعر. يبقى إدخال ما قبل الدفع في الذاكرة حسب السياسة؛ لا حفظ نشاط مؤقت مخالف لها.
4. **الدفع:** على الموقع نفسه بوضع Paymob Test المعتمد؛ تأكيد خادمي موثوق، إنشاء واحد بعد التحقق، وعدم تكرار الطلب عند الرجوع أو التحديث. غياب التفويض أو الإعداد يبقي البوابة معلقة ولا يبرر تغيير حالة الدفع يدويًا.
5. **تجهيز الفرع المدفوع:** ساعات العمل واللغة والمعرفة؛ حفظ ثم تحديث الصفحة ومطابقة البيانات في الفرع نفسه. لا تُقبل أزرار غير موصولة. دعوة الفريق اختيارية فقط إذا كان مسار التخطي المعتمد مثبتًا.
6. **المساعد والقناة:** سؤال معلوم من معرفة الفرع ثم رسالة من رقمنا للقناة المصرح بها؛ جواب مناسب مرة واحدة، دليل إرسال وتسوية صحيحان، وقياس الزمن. تُنفذ بعد توافر تفويض المكالمات/الرسائل وتكلفتها؛ لا اختلاق رد أو تجاهل UNKNOWN.
7. **العزل والصلاحيات:** شركتان مخصصتان لنا، محاولة قراءة/تعديل مورد الشركة الأخرى وفتح رابطها المباشر؛ المنع مثبت من الخادم وليس إخفاء رابط فقط. قبول رفض لا يثبت وحده كل RLS؛ مراجعة العقد المتأثر تظل لازمة.
8. **الهاتف والإطلاق المحدود:** إكمال المسار الأساسي من الهاتف، مراجعة الأخطاء والأزمنة الفعلية والدعم/الاسترداد، ثم إتاحة مدعوين محدودين بموافقة الإطلاق بعد إغلاق البوابات المطلوبة. لا إلغاء لمتطلبات المنتج المتبقية أو SMTP/المزودين.

ميزانية الاستجابة المقترحة للمراجعة وليست قياسات حالية أو وعد SLA: إظهار استجابة للضغط خلال 200ms، والصفحات/الحفظ العادي خلال 2s في اتصال المراجعة. زمن جواب المساعد يقاس منفصلًا من استقبال الرسالة حتى قبول الإرسال مع بيان زمن المزود؛ يحدد حد قبوله بعد أول قياس حقيقي. البطء الظاهر يعالج في خطوته دون مشروع تحسين منفصل.

التحقق لا يتحول إلى حملة اختبارات: تعديل وثائق = فحص diff؛ إصلاح منطقي = فحص مباشر مركز عند الحاجة؛ تعديل مالي/صلاحيات/تزامن = إثبات الحماية المتأثرة قبل تعريضها للعملاء؛ CI والبناء المطلوبان عند الإصدار لا يُتجاوزان. لا ننفذ عمدًا عمليات تدميرية أو تكرار خصم/إرسال على بيانات حقيقية. عند مانع خارجي ننجز فقط الخطوات المستقلة ونبقي البوابة معلقة؛ لا تقدم صوري.

الخطوة العملية التالية: تثبيت الرابط والنسخة المنشورة والكاتب الحالي (خطوة 1)، ثم تقديم حركة واحدة للمستخدم في كل مرة بدل إرسال قائمة تنفيذ كاملة. هذا الطلب لم ينفذ جولة لايف أو نشرًا بعد.

## Single-branch current scope & explicit multi-branch deferral — 2026-10-21

- **Current Scope**: Velora currently operates strictly with **one branch per business (activity)**.
- **Multi-branch Support Explicitly Deferred**: Multi-branch support is formally deferred to a post-launch phase. Existing multi-branch database structures, branch columns, enterprise isolation, permissions, and tables are preserved intact and must not be deleted, stripped, or weakened.
- **Data Integrity & Non-Conversion**: Existing branch records, historical multi-branch test fixtures, and company data are never auto-converted, merged, re-parented, or deleted.
- **Enforcement & User Transparency**:
  - Creation of a second or additional active/setup branch for an existing company is blocked at the server/RPC/action boundary with explicit user-facing notice (`SECOND_BRANCH_DEFERRED`).
  - Native trigger guard `private.es02_single_branch_guard` in migration `20261020150000_es02_single_branch_scope.sql` ensures database-level enforcement without disrupting tenant isolation or existing unit test mocks.
  - Multi-branch completion must not be claimed; the application remains truthfully scoped to single-branch operational readiness.

## Current onboarding decision replacement — 2026-10-19, 19:49 UTC

Authority: revised specification Sentinel_2a5162463d348191b6c800ddb7126bd5 (19:48 UTC), explicitly confirmed as a decision by Sentinel_1d40f7cdfec48191afe89eb9fc132753 (19:49 UTC). This supersedes only the earlier recommendation principle/mapping proposal, deferred website-learning quota proposal, and campaign balance currency. It does not advance the ES02 stage or change the verified foundation.

- Persona recommendation is a badge only, based solely on declared team size: 1–3 → Noura; 4–10 → Lina; more than 10 → Dana. Both 11–25 and more than 25 select Dana. Goals and channels do not determine the badge. The customer may freely choose another persona. Do not recommend Standard/basic versus Premium, change prices, grant entitlements or imply the recommended persona can accommodate the team.
- Capacity remains per purchased branch: Standard/basic includes the owner plus 2 employees and 1 channel; Premium includes the owner plus 4 employees and 2 channels. The owner is excluded from the employee allowance. Show this capacity truth separately from the persona badge; a Dana badge for a team larger than 10 is not a capacity promise. Subscription prices, currency, term and immutable branch rights remain unchanged.
- Website learning quota is approved at 10 pages for basic and 50 pages for Premium. This replaces the deferred 20/100 proposal. Website input remains optional and learning begins after payment. Page counting, scope, enforcement and import-cost implementation remain pending; the approved numbers are not pending. These learning limits are distinct from the existing Velora website creation addon of up to 5 pages.
- Campaign prepaid balance is denominated in USD, replacing the earlier OMR proposal. It remains separate from subscription charges and AI reply credits. Show campaign cost before launch. Campaign pricing, refunds, currency conversion and provider integration remain later-stage work. Subscription currency is unchanged.
- Preserve the approved sequence: local business data and questions → plan → review → payment → create company and branches → connect channels and assistant learning. No free service trial before payment. Answers stay in current-visit memory before payment and are saved to company data only after verified payment, with isolation and permissions; that storage decision remains approved despite the revised pasted specification calling it open. The minimal schema/provisioning implementation remains pending.
- Image preview approval is still required before the three-screen UI implementation. Social-link ban claims and +968 coexistence eligibility remain unverified; do not assert them or introduce a Meta-mandated domain block.

Documentation change only: no product code, migrations, tests, Cursor package, runtime change, Production, commit or push. Preserve the completed 37/37 foundation tests, TypeScript and lint evidence. The team wording and company-wide workforce scope are settled by the 19:56 clarification. No recommendation scoring or mixed-goal persona algorithm remains to approve: the team-size badge rule above is decided.

#### Bounded QuickWins and medium-large package order

QuickWins-proposed: complete availability labeling for every displayed feature (absence of badge not success); consistent Standard/basic/persona/quota terminology; expose existing source/version/pending evidence; align errors/duplicate-click/refresh-recovery. Each needs its own scoped UI/server proof and cannot substitute for CF functionality, alter the commercial promise or hide a missing service. New event names/collector coverage not invented.

1. **Commercial contract reconciliation** (ES02/PF02/PF03/PF07): sixspec/twelveterm matrix + financial offer revisions + field/key/availability/limit mappings, preserve documented prices/features and legacyhistory. Exit independent offer/feature/binding evidence, not12fake buy buttons.
2. **Purchase/rights/credits integration** (ES02+ES04,ES03 consumers): paid provisioning, monthly/annual/branch/seats/channels/quotas and separate extra replies; approved priority/rollover + unresolved prices/bundles/scope. One financial/usage writer; no excluded current-cycle-upgrade alias.
3. **Complete persona service vertical** (ES03): actual agreed persona/channel functions, every includedCF independently accepted; current customer AI gates closed. No assumed autonomous commerce behavior or deferral by difficulty.
4. **Operator/lifecycle/reporting integration** (ES04/PF08/PF10): review/support/alerts/report definitions, renew/cancel/refund/recovery + promised persona report outcomes. Retain financial receipts and approvedscope, no fictional aggregate success.

### Architecture sources and verification limits

Supplied official research informs proposals; it does not establish current Velora enforcement or approve business thresholds. No AWS infrastructure, new queue service, agent-builder product, separate test project/page or paid call is selected by citation.

- Durability/idempotency: [Next after duration](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/api-reference/functions/after), [Supabase queues/visibility](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/queues), [transactional outbox pattern](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/en_en/prescriptive-guidance/latest/cloud-design-patterns/transactional-outbox.html), [safe idempotent retries](https://aws.amazon.com/builders-library/making-retries-safe-with-idempotent-APIs/), [tenant fairness pattern](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/en_en/AWSSimpleQueueService/latest/SQSDeveloperGuide/sqs-fair-queues.html). Visibility window is not external exactly-once; technical queue choice remains proposed.
- Version/recovery/data: [deploymentId](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/api-reference/config/next-config-js/deploymentId), [migrations](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/deployment/database-migrations), [environments](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/deployment/managing-environments), [DB backups exclude Storage](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/platform/backups), [clone scope](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/platform/clone-project), [user deletion/session/Storage boundaries](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/auth/managing-user-data), [Pino redaction](https://github.com/pinojs/pino/blob/main/docs/redaction.md).
- RAG/AI: [RLS](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/database/postgres/row-level-security), [RAG permissions](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/ai/rag-with-permissions), [prompt injection controls](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/LLM_Prompt_Injection_Prevention_Cheat_Sheet.html), [agent-builder safety principles](https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/guides/agent-builder-safety) (legacy product page, principles only), [spend limits](https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/guides/spend-limits), [rate limits](https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/guides/rate-limits), [evaluation](https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/guides/evaluation-best-practices), [deprecations](https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/deprecations). Provider hardspend may lag; Velora branch budget is server authority; evaluations and model changes remain approval/cost gated.
- Data/secrets/UX: [file upload](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/File_Upload_Cheat_Sheet.html), [SSRF](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Server_Side_Request_Forgery_Prevention_Cheat_Sheet.html), [provider data controls](https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/guides/your-data), [secret lifecycle](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Secrets_Management_Cheat_Sheet.html), [SLO alerting](https://sre.google/workbook/alerting-on-slos/), [accessible status](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/status-messages.html), [error identification](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/error-identification.html), [timing](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/timing-adjustable.html).

### Proposed flow objectives and first execution identifiers

The ten functional Flow objectives/five-stage structure are approved as architectural direction at2026-10-15 22:55UTC. Detailed task fields, timing, repair scope and acceptance execution remain proposed. No row authorizes programming or testing.

| Flow | Exact objective |
|---|---|
| PF01 | Establish evidence-backed shared identity, session, tenant/branch authorization and recovery contracts while reusing existing native authorities. |
| PF02 | Move a prospective customer through truthful discovery, verified account and valid local business/branch preparation without prohibited prepayment persistence. |
| PF03 | Turn an authoritative financial offer and verified Paymob purchase into exactly-once paid company/branch provisioning and authenticated no-recharge recovery. |
| PF04 | Persist and recover paid business settings, staff configuration and generation-bound knowledge/review with explicit authority and conflict handling. |
| PF05 | Connect, verify and recover each entitled official channel with correct customer assets, provider grants and actual authorized message evidence. |
| PF06 | Operate isolated branch/staff inbox, human handoff, advertised services and approved AI with final-dispatch, quota and unknown-outcome controls. |
| PF07 | Enforce group activation, cycle quotas, renewal/cancellation/reactivation and financial/customer-exit rights without duplicate money or entitlement effects. |
| PF08 | Deliver scoped platform-team and operator workflows for companies, reviews, finance, models, support, tasks and audit using authoritative domain contracts. |
| PF09 | Preserve and implement separately decided commercial expansion through real supplier, consent, cost, permission and operational capability contracts. |
| PF10 | Prove affected end-to-end behavior, diagnosis, security gates, exact release and lawful data recovery continuously, without false readiness claims. |

First proposed cross-flow anchor: **PF01 (PF10 co-owner) / ES01-S01 / WP01A**. ES01-S01 is the proposed first cross-flow shared-authority/evidence-contract stage (earlier draft PF01-S01 remains a historical proposed-label crosswalk); WP01A is the existing medium/large contract-consolidation package, not a new microtask. Reason: downstream purchase/provisioning/staff/channel/AI decisions require a current actor/session/tenant/branch authority and durable recovery semantics. This first package identifies proven reuse and specific gaps (logout pending deadline/activity semantics/native wrappers/workspace revocation), rather than rebuilding identity or applying the historical lock. Follow with WP01B boundary/recovery integration only after approved contracts and execution authorization. Stage acceptance is source-backed caller/RPC/data-scope matrix and approved measurable boundary/recovery criteria, then separately authorized runtime proof; neither source presence nor this document marks it Done.

### Proposed Paymob Sandbox acceptance on existing product routes

Status **proposed**, date2026-10-15, source latest user request plus supplied Paymob research/static audit. Use the **two already-authorized test accounts only**, with identities/roles/ownership and available login method verified from permitted targeted evidence first; do not assume emails, passwords, sessions or create another account. No tests are executed or credentials inspected by this documentation edit. The supplied narrow static config/route audit and official Sandbox research are integrated below; active mode/isolation/account binding remain unverified pre-execution gates.

- **Actual product, no separate test project/page:** begin from existing auth/account/workspace and `src/app/account/businesses/new/page.tsx` / `src/app/setup/page.tsx`, actual catalog/checkout actions and `src/app/api/billing/paymob/webhook/route.ts`. Route availability and real controls must be mapped from code. The local preparation currently stops with payment unavailable and checkout requires company: legacy company-bound Sandbox proof would validate only that subset, NOT new pre-company paid provisioning. Missing target journey must remain blocked until approved implementation exists; no fake checkout or bypass to manufacture a pass.
- **Isolation gate before any future test:** positively identify merchant/integration/environment/payment mode and callback destinations, ensure no real card/charge/recurring mandate/payment-link/customer notification or production entitlement/data mutation. Test credentials must stay in approved secret storage; no values copied into Brain/logs/browser code. Use only an authorized safely isolated existing runtime/fixtures if available. If the current runtime points to production business/financial data or cannot prevent real effects, mark BLOCKED and request an approved isolation approach; do not silently create a new project, change services/config or grant sandbox payments production rights.
- **Account A/B authority:** after identities are verified, prove A owns its allowed purchase/company/branch slots; B cannot read/resume/claim/provision/refund or receive A data through UI/direct action/RPC/callback-return tampering. Preserve both accounts' unrelated existing memberships and paid records. Account switch/open tabs invalidate previous context. No granted role escalation or new customer invitation inferred.
- **Happy-path evidence:** actual existing UI -> approved immutable offer/one term/branch slots -> Sandbox attempt -> authoritative processed POST HMAC verification -> verified purchase state -> (when implemented) exactly-one company/branches/receipt -> real settings reload/resume. GET return is display only; amount/currency/merchant/integration/kind checks and correct expected actor are required. Lost browser business input after payment is re-entered against verified paid purchase, never a second checkout.
- **Failure/recovery evidence:** decline/cancel/pending/unknown timeout, refresh/back/forward/double click/tabs, delayed/duplicate/out-of-order/same-transaction-later-state callback, forged return/wrong ownership/amount/currency/environment, double-success liability, provisioning partial/crash/retry, auth expiry/offline and account switch. Use provider-supported Sandbox scenarios/authorized callback replay only after their exact capability is audited; never edit financial truth or production clocks to fake them. Refund/recurring cases remain separately policy/capability-gated; token alone is not consent.
- **Measurable result manifest:** one stable scenario ID per expected invariant with account alias (not contact secret), environment/merchant-integration alias, app SHA/schema version, actual route/action/RPC, expected/actual authoritative state/receipt, correlation/provider event reference, duration, sanitized failure, observed effect count and owned-fixture cleanup outcome. A pass requires actual server/DB proof and UI confirmation; accepted provider request != delivered settlement or unique provisioning. Report planned/executed/passed/failed/blocked/unrun over a frozen manifest denominator, with partial legacy scope separated from complete target journey.
- **Stop/recovery:** unknown outcome reconciles via inquiry/same reducer before new charge; duplicate effect/foreign access/environment mismatch stops the run and preserves redacted evidence. Cleanup touches only explicitly owned authorized Sandbox fixtures, never unrelated real financial/customer records. No destructive cleanup, real refund/charge or production entitlement repair is authorized by this plan.

No provider/browser calls, config/credential-file reads, tests or mutations ran in either this document edit or the supplied narrow audit. Never repoint a production receiver for testing. Sandbox provider mode does not isolate DB/email/workers/entitlements. Existing test success explicitly grants no paid live service.

#### Proposed existing-page Sandbox scenario manifest

Manifest version **proposal2026-10-15**; denominator **12 proposed existing company-bound cases**, not12 executed tests or whole product acceptance. Every case is **UNRUN**, with execution blocked until plan approval, named-account scope and redacted configuration/isolation preflight. Optional provider decline/replay/injected-fault cases retain conditional capability labels. Unsupported cases are blocked/unrun, not failed or passed. Freeze a run-specific applicable denominator before any future authorized run; report excluded cases/reasons separately.

| Case | Existing route/action | Required authoritative evidence / condition |
|---|---|---|
| SB01 | A plan -> startTestCheckoutAction -> persisted order/status | Correct test account/company/offer/OMR order/attempt/processed receipt, one legitimate Sandbox settlement effect; no live money/grant/Meta/AI action |
| SB02 | B independent plan/collection | Verified separate B scope/history, A unchanged; no assumption of actual identity or payment authorization |
| SB03 | A/B foreign order open/resume/stale selected-company | Denied disclosure/resumeURL/mutation across companies and user change; current native/session authority |
| SB04 | Account/workspace switch mid-request on panel/status | Late A response cannot render/redirect under B; no old secret URL/data |
| SB05 | Double click/tabs/reload same selection/operation | One legitimate attempt/intention; persisted checkout reused; unresolved claim blocks another collection |
| SB06 | Provider decline/failed3DS conditionally supported | Recorded provider evidence, no entitlement/new charge. Exact documented fixture/method required; not assumed from generic test cards |
| SB07 | Cancel/back/close/3DS interruption | Browser event is not proof of failure; honest pending/unknown, no premature unlock/recharge |
| SB08 | Delayed/missing callback/status refresh/reconcile | Pending honest; authoritative later result converges once, GET query never grants success; page processing treated as mutation |
| SB09 | Duplicate/out-of-order/same-transaction later callback | Approved isolated replay mechanism only; one effect, valid later transitions retained, no duplicate rights/time |
| SB10 | Timeout/unknown provider outcome | Authorized controlled scenario; uncertainty persisted, inquiry/same reducer before new charge; simulated faults labeled as such |
| SB11 | Lost response/return/post-payment reload/resume | Same persisted test purchase recovered, no new order/intention. Exact processed effect/receipt linked to status; no live service |
| SB12 | Mode/merchant/integration/amount/currency mismatch | Isolated approved fixture boundary, reject live/wrong receipt and no settlement; test flag alone insufficient |

**Separate target-journey manifest,4 proposed cases (UNRUN, implementation absent/gated):** TJ01 refresh before payment starts blank/no server business draft or auto-restore; TJ02 B abandons preparation/checkout with no business creation or entitlement; TJ03 A verified Sandbox purchase -> exactly-one sandbox-only entitlement/provisioning receipt/company/slots, lost return/input -> authenticated billing recovery and re-entry without new checkout; TJ04 paid partial provisioning/application crash -> retry original paid purchase, no recharge, B cannot claim A purchase. These are future isolated real-page acceptance of PF02/PF03 contracts, NOT validated by legacy company-bound SB cases. They must never grant production service, live Meta/AI or real customer entitlements. Current new-business local review has no payment handoff, so do not bypass it or invent a test page to run them.

Measured execution in this task: **0/12 existing-path cases and0/4 target cases executed**; this is an execution count, not0% historical product verification or a passing/failing coverage score. There is no provider failure result and no real-payment Sandbox readiness assertion. First implementation anchor is PF01 with PF10 / ES01-S01 / WP01A; Sandbox acceptance is later proposed verification after its dependencies and explicit authorization.

### Domain dependencies supporting the execution slices

This is the domain dependency graph supporting ES01-ES05 above, not a competing sequence of ten execution stages. Shared invariants/owners are defined once in ARC and function cards; individual PF sections below retain detailed product coverage.

1. PF01 shared identity/tenant/branch/session/recovery contracts first, reusing existing authorities. PF10 release/observability/recovery design starts alongside this as a cross-cutting lane, not a postponed final cleanup.
2. After PF01 contract review, PF02 customer preparation and PF08 platform/team authority can proceed in independent lanes. PF03 financial contracts may be designed in parallel, but purchase/provisioning integration waits for PF02's boundary and approved financial inputs.
3. PF03 verified settlement and recovery precede PF04 paid configuration/knowledge and PF07 operational subscription lifecycle. PF05 provider adapters may be prepared independently after PF01; actual customer activation requires PF03 entitlements plus applicable PF04 approvals.
4. PF06 messaging/staff/AI operation integrates PF04 + PF05 and PF07 quota authority. PF08 operator surfaces integrate existing bounded domain APIs rather than bypassing them.
5. PF09 expands retained commercial services only after each supplier/price/consent/capability contract is decided; it is part of full scope, not an inferred owner deferral. Its priority remains an explicit approval item.
6. PF10 accepts exact candidate changes incrementally; complete customer/operator/recovery journeys and external gates determine release readiness. Independent parallel work cannot grant permission to mutate production or buy resources.

Shared contract gate: expected actor/session/account/company/branch IDs, action capability, entitlement proof, revision/generation, idempotency key, correlation IDs, stable errors, effect receipt and unknown-outcome semantics. Freeze interfaces before independent edits. Auth/tenant/grants changes, billing/activation/usage and channel/dispatch contracts each have a single coordinated writer; serialize their integration. UI and domain lanes can proceed against a frozen contract, but adapter/mock success cannot establish production readiness. Actual future tool/agent availability must be confirmed; this document dispatches nobody.

### Shared UX journey and readiness contract (PROPOSED)

Source: parent-supplied separately authorized GPT-6 Astra Medium read-only review parts1-3,2026-10-15. No model invocation by this writer/browser/tests. Source observations differ from untested visual behavior. Existing routes/ui.tsx/CSS tokens retained. Catalog+financial intent -> verified account -> blank memory-only preparation -> immutable financial review -> payment->settlement->once-only provisioning->paid setup; authenticate owned paid recovery BEFORE offering another checkout, independent company existence. Staff direct scoped invitation and platform admin authority separate. Paid details/staff/knowledge/entitled-channel tasks parallel with explicit dependencies. See DIAGNOSTICS shared action matrix and9unrun UX scenarios, not a new project/report/page.

UX journey ordering is PROPOSED, not an approved commercial sequence. Optional prelogin selection intent is bounded/non-sensitive (catalog package/term identifiers only, revalidated after auth), never unpaid business/branch field data, server business records or restored drafts. Every completion percentage must state numerator, denominator, scope and authoritative revision: profile/branch completion measures business details only, not operational readiness. No guessed weighted global percentage. Purchased-first/carry-until-used/no ordinary expiry/separate balances and23:41 captured-session quarantine/server-revocation/newer-session isolation are ALREADY APPROVED, not UX choices to reopen.

All action states: available, blocked-with-reason, pending, confirmed, partial, unknown, failed; suppress duplicate effect, status reconcile unknown, saved+refresh-failed remains saved. Back/cancel/windowclose does not reverse committed effect. Completion labels distinguish Business details complete, Payment confirmed, Knowledge approved, Channel connected and Replies ready. Existing wizard8/mobile /8/profile+branches100% is static competing-model risk, not tested rendered defect; no blanket Setup complete as operational proof. Tester ALREADY has upfront unavailable notice; RETAIN it, null-provider is not operational acceptance.

Navigation groups existing authorized destinations into Overview/Conversations/Knowledge/Channels/Team-Branches/Subscription/Settings; Flows not ten tabs, personal/admin distinct. Prominent business/branch/role, block old-context actions during switch, shared guarded logout, same native-session isolation. Reuse sidebar/mobile menu/deeplinks/inbox filters; no new bottom-nav hierarchy. AR/EN logical layout/bidi/icon/focus/field errors/live states/touch/keyboard/text zoom proposed; visual contrast/clipping/responsiveness UNTESTED. Cursor state/semantic/server owner, Antigravity frozen disjoint presentation using current components, Codex later authorized verification. Truthful journey first; decorative polish optional afterward. No added price or included-feature deferral.

### PF01 - Shared identity, tenant/branch authority and recovery foundation

**Packages:** WP01A evidence-backed authority contract consolidation; WP01B lifecycle/recovery and cross-boundary enforcement. Dependencies: source audit and approved contract decisions. Reuse auth/session-context/idle-session, tenancy access/actions, enterprise grants and existing database authorities; no wholesale identity rebuild.

- **UI/UX:** shared guarded logout, password-commit form replaced by revocation recovery, captured quarantine/newer-session isolation; reliable identity/workspace chooser, owner/staff destinations, pending/error/retry/re-auth states; no stale prior-account content. Accessible Arabic/English return paths.
- **Backend/database:** one documented actor/session/tenant/branch capability contract across Actions/API/RPC/workers; authoritative memberships and branch ownership, versioned mutation receipts and session state. Check direct and legacy consumers before proposing schema changes.
- **Security/integrations:** least-privilege RLS, Storage/queues/realtime/cache/export boundaries; native Auth remains identity provider. Membership is not paid entitlement. Sensitive operations require strict current-session checks; do not assume logout immediately invalidates JWTs or trust user_metadata/old membership claims. No shared-cache authenticated responses.
- **Logging/recovery:** allowlisted identity/capability outcome + correlation, no raw cookies/tokens/contact data; revoke/re-auth and unavailable versus unauthenticated remain distinct. Fence late responses and retain legitimate unrelated sessions.
- **Acceptance/E2E gate:** direct Action/API/RPC/Storage/worker denial for foreign user/company/branch, revoked/stale sessions and changed grants; owner/staff/workspace switching, two tabs, refresh/back/forward, expiry/offline/retry. Contract and recovery evidence must cover real server boundaries before dependent package acceptance.

### PF02 - Discovery, verified account and local business preparation

**Packages:** WP02A truthful discovery/identity journey; WP02B memory-only business/branch preparation and explicit local suggestions. Depends on PF01; financial handoff contract agreed with PF03.

- **UI/UX:** unchanged six-package intent through auth, native form and per-field NOT SAVED feedback; continue existing preparation into financial review, paid recovery branch independent company existence. Approved prices/personas/videos, signup/verification/profile, blank return fields, focus-only explicitly selected suggestions; required business phone, primary category, country/city and branch name/city; one/multiple branches independently reviewed. No automatic full-draft restore. Accessible step errors/loading/cancel/review/back states.
- **Backend/database:** reuse confirmed account/profile flow; before payment persist only permitted identity and financial records, never business setup or branch fields. Local preparation does not call legacy creation/draft/save paths. Business content must not leak through URL/native submit, metadata, logs, analytics or checkout payload.
- **Security/integrations:** account-scoped suggestions with explicit save/delete and privacy limits; SMTP/Google/SMS configuration is separate verified work, not an assumed provider approval.
- **Logging/recovery:** safe validation codes only; lost local form returns blank, and payment recovery uses PF03's verified purchase rather than restoring a server business draft.
- **Acceptance/E2E:** invalid/duplicate branches, changed phone edits, double-click/tab, account switch, refresh/history, popup cancellation and expired auth; prove no server business-write or hidden full-form persistence before payment. Dana media remains waiting; no generated paid substitute inferred.

### PF03 - Trusted offer, payment and exactly-once paid provisioning

**Packages:** WP03A immutable per-branch offer/order/settlement authority; WP03B paid-purchase recovery and atomic company/branch provisioning. Depends on PF01 + PF02; provider/merchant/pricing/legal decisions must be explicit.

- **UI/UX:** existing account/setup locates owned paid purchase before another checkout; show perbranch total/term/monthly allowance/consent, returning provider != paymentconfirmed. Independent branch persona/tier with one monthly/annual term per order, real totals/consent, pending/failed/unknown payment; authenticated paid-purchase recovery and re-entry of lost setup without another checkout.
- **Backend/database:** separate payment, provisioning and channel-readiness state machines. Proposed provisioning states: paid_unprovisioned -> provisioning -> provisioned or failed-recoverable. Minimal billing-only records before settlement; unique business effects, ownership/amount/currency/environment checks, durable receipts, concurrency locks and replay-safe reconciliation. States/table names are proposals, not mandated schema.
- **Security/integrations:** verify selected provider's actual signature/body semantics, merchant and financial evidence; never trust return URLs. The static audit confirms Paymob in the inspected implementation; live configuration and merchant acceptance remain unverified. Use the Paymob-specific authority/acceptance section below; no alternative provider selected. No business setup in provider metadata/logs/server drafts.
- **Logging/recovery:** separate financial audit from operational logs; acknowledge durable webhook acceptance, handle duplicate/out-of-order callbacks, delayed settlement and reversals. Reconcile unknown charges before retry; never recharge a verified paid purchase to recover lost input.
- **Acceptance/E2E:** forged prices/slots/ownership, parallel attempts/tabs, duplicate/delayed callbacks, browser lost after payment, partial provisioning/crash/retry and reversal preserve one legitimate effect/receipt and financial liabilities. Real charge/refund remains separately authorized.

### PF04 - Paid business configuration, people, knowledge and review

**Packages:** WP04A durable paid settings/branches/staff setup; WP04B generation-bound knowledge/assistant configuration and review. Depends on PF03; people authority contracts come from PF01 and runtime dispatch from PF06.

- **UI/UX:** parallel paid tasks with explicit dependencies; label businessdetails/payment/knowledge/channel/replies readiness separately. Existing product configuration for contacts/maps/hours/holidays, products/services and branches, employee/invitation permissions, knowledge/files and assistant settings; truthful saved/unsaved/conflict/error states and owner-reviewer-owner resubmission.
- **Backend/database:** reuse company IDs, settings/autosave/CAS and invitation receipts. Native confirmed-company persistence only after paid provisioning; immutable submission/file generations and approved retrieval versions. Preserve legacy paid history; retire conflicting consumers only after compatible replacement and traceable data handling.
- **Security/integrations:** owner/manager/seat/branch limits on every server path; Storage ownership/MIME/size/generation checks; review is distinct from channel/payment/AI readiness. No stale reviewer decisions authorizing changed content.
- **Logging/recovery:** revision/attempt/review/file outcome references; recover lost saves/uploads without silent overwrite, duplicate invitations or path-only destructive cleanup. Retention/legal deletion requires PF10 reviewed policy.
- **Acceptance/E2E:** actual reload persistence, concurrent edit/conflict, invitation reissue/cancel/revoke, staff ceiling, late upload after cancellation, reviewed-generation mismatch and account switch; only authorized complete business settings count as acceptance.

### PF05 - Official channel onboarding, connection and provider recovery

**Packages:** WP05A WhatsApp/Instagram entitlement-bound onboarding/reconnect; WP05B separately scoped Messenger and review/provider approval journey. Depends on PF01; real activation gated by PF03/PF04.

- **UI/UX:** per-branch entitled channel readiness, actual grants/assets/errors, popup cancellation and resumable reconnect; provider approval, customer connection and working send/receive have separate states.
- **Backend/database:** reuse native binding/secret-reference/event-generation authorities; isolate WhatsApp, Instagram and Messenger identities/scopes/windows. Store scoped receipts and binding revisions; no global-token fallback or cross-tenant reuse.
- **Security/integrations:** verify exact current Meta coexistence/Tech Provider/customer-onboarding permissions at launch. Current direct Meta developer pages returned HTTP429; accessible Meta-owned reference/Postman evidence and remaining exact gates are distinguished in Meta foundational acceptance02:19 below. No number deletion/transfer or customer messaging inferred from planning.
- **Logging/recovery:** safe grant/binding/generation/error references; bounded retries and explicit expired/revoked/partial authorization. Reconcile ambiguous provider effects; preserve existing numbers and valid paid setup.
- **Acceptance/E2E:** cancel/deny/partial scopes/wrong asset/expired token/revocation, duplicate webhooks, reconnect and native sync; actual authorized inbound/outbound receipts for each channel. Popup success and review test access cannot certify customer activation.

Meta foundational acceptance02:19 below supplies the bounded current source/research integration and staged WP05A-B/WP06A-B/WP10A-B repair briefs; source code and reviewer sends are not operational proof.

### PF06 - Branch staff, omnichannel inbox, AI and service operation

**Packages:** WP06A branch/shift/presence/assignment and human inbox lifecycle; WP06B approved AI/service execution with usage reservation and final dispatch. Depends on PF01 + PF04 + PF05 + PF07 quota/entitlement contracts.

- **UI/UX:** scoped inbox/search/attachments/notes/tags/leads, assignments/handoff, close/reopen/rating, human reply and truthful delivery/uncertainty; Arabic/English/mobile/keyboard, role and branch views.
- **Backend/database:** reuse durable inbound/outbound intents and conversation revisions; least-loaded assignment/shift/recovery, claim/generation-bound workers and exactly-once usage effects. Authorized approved knowledge/model/service policy and current entitlement/quota required at final send, not just UI entry.
- **Security/integrations:** recheck revoked staff, branch binding, provider window and capability before dispatch; no leakage through counts/search/realtime/attachments/exports. AI budget and optional services are independent gates; preserve authorized human ingress/reply when AI is depleted.
- **Logging/recovery:** correlate inbound -> assignment -> retrieval -> reservation -> provider outcome, redact before emission. Durable bounded retry/backoff; unknown send outcome requires reconciliation rather than blind resend. Preserve thread history and original generation.
- **Acceptance/E2E:** competing staff/worker takeover, revoked grant just before send, duplicate inbound, out-of-window/opt-out, missing knowledge/quota/channel, network outage and uncertain send; prove tenant/branch isolation, no duplicate dispatch/charge and actual service behavior beyond marketing labels.

### PF07 - Subscription groups, renewal, quota, accounting and customer exit

**Packages:** WP07A activation/group clock/quota lifecycle; WP07B renewal/cancellation/reactivation/financial liability and exit. Depends on PF03 and PF05 readiness contracts; data deletion relies on PF10 approved recovery/retention.

- **UI/UX:** renewal-only baseplan and independent Buy extra replies with distinct balances/effective dates/receipts; CycleCreditSummary cycle-expiry copy not approved purchased policy. Separate paid/entitled/channel/AI states, common order start/end, first-purchase week, monthly quotas on either term, renewal branch choice and mandate/cancellation/grace/expiry/recovery disclosures. Package/persona/term changes at renewal only.
- **Backend/database:** 23:57 approved INITIAL paid clock starts only on coherent first-channel AND actual AI readiness; normal renewal starts dayone without another waiting window; atomic original-order group barrier described in Diagnostics; seven-day deadline excludes proven provider/Velora delay. Bonus once for eligible first successful purchase across its branches; later branches independent. Normalrenewal starts dayone of scheduled newcycle without readiness waiting;3day failuregrace separate, no second bonus or extra grace charge; late-reactivation start not inferred. Idempotent cycle/reset/reservation ledger; independent extra AI reply purchases approved23:04 and refined23:13: purchased-first, no ordinary expiry, carry until used across cycles, separate purchased/base balances, no package/subscription-expiry change. Only price/bundle/scope/refund/compensation and spending eligibility after base expiration remain unresolved; preserve owned balance, no automatic overage sale.
- **Security/integrations:** current explicit recurring mandate, verified amount/currency/provider outcome, immutable invoice/refund/wallet liabilities and owner access. Exact retry/notice/refund/legal policies unresolved where unsourced. Do not reuse superseded immediate-upgrade rules.
- **Logging/recovery:** financial audit for charge/settlement/refund/grant, separate measured versus estimated AI cost; reconcile unknown renewal before new charge. Three-day grace on failure, bounded attempts; cancellation retains earned time. Reactivation preserves setup but revalidates permissions/channels.
- **Acceptance/E2E:** monthly/annual/multiple branches, first/later purchase, shared proven outage, renewal race/manual-vs-auto, cancel-before-charge, missed webhook, expiry and depleted quotas; preserve ledger money/rights. Shared-outage time extension and older AI-credit compensation require distinct approved rules; no invented formula or double grant.

### PF08 - Platform teams, administration and operator workflows

**Packages:** WP08A internal hierarchy/delegation/team transfer and account/company administration; WP08B review/catalog/finance/model/support/task/health operations. Depends on PF01, then relevant domain APIs PF03-PF07; finance mutation integration serialized.

- **UI/UX:** real owner/delegated rosters, revisions and transfer review; company/account detail/history/search/filters/export, reviews, offers, financial evidence, model policies, official Velora support inbox/tickets, notifications/tasks/saved views/daily reports and controlled settings. Labels must reflect availability.
- **Backend/database:** reuse existing platform-team/access/billing/catalog readers and mutation receipts; scoped pagination, durable notification dedupe/recipient/snooze, audit and policy draft/publish/rollback. Internal role grants no customer-company membership.
- **Security/integrations:** least-privilege role/manager scope; explicit support delegation and two-person controls where approved; authentic provider/model metrics, no fake aggregate counters or unauthorized customer actions.
- **Logging/recovery:** operator action/revision/receipt and security audit; recover ambiguous saves with the same key, incident/queue triage and configuration rollback without exposing secrets or discarding customer data.
- **Acceptance/E2E:** owner/manager/employee/foreign tenant, concurrent transfer, unknown save/replay, filters/back/forward, actual report drill-down, notification lifecycle and support workflow; current exact-runtime acceptance separate from old team reports.

### PF09 - Retained commercial capabilities and expansion

**Packages:** WP09A supplier/price/consent/limits and specialist capability contracts; WP09B independently scoped working service adapters and operator/customer journeys. Depends on PF01 + PF03 + PF06 + PF07. Full approved scope retained; priority and business contracts need approval, not silent deferral.

- **UI/UX:** truthful per-persona capability catalog and optional website/store/POS/stock/accounting/workflows, voice, generated content/campaigns and specialist/admin/partner/White Label flows; preview/approval/handoff and setup/recurring cost disclosures.
- **Backend/database:** reuse service allowlist, consent/version/usage/order authorities; persistent request/job/output/approval receipts and tenant-scoped configuration. No standalone invented subsystem before existing capability audit.
- **Security/integrations:** supplier identities, credentials, paid budgets, legal recording/retention, publication scopes and two-person approvals explicit; unsupported automatic WhatsApp Status stays unverified. Provider permissions and customer approval are distinct.
- **Logging/recovery:** allowlisted job/provider/approval/usage references, bounded retries and unknown-effect reconciliation; partial publishing/booking/order repair preserves financial and customer data.
- **Acceptance/E2E:** prove each advertised operation, scoped persistence, manual fallback, cancellation/retry/approval, actual supported provider publishing/telephony/store effect and cost ledger. A video/mock/demo cannot establish telephony, accounting or workflow functionality.

### PF10 - Continuous verification, release, diagnostics and safe data recovery

**Packages:** WP10A required CI/security/observability/release proof alongside each flow; WP10B export/retention/erasure/backup/restore/incident operations. Cross-cutting from PF01; final release depends on accepted affected flows and separately authorized external configuration.

- **UI/UX:** accessible errors and safe diagnostic references in actual existing product routes, clear destructive confirmations/status and recovery instructions; no separate test projects/pages. Historical QA-center requests are retained, not permission to create a QA surface or security bypass.
- **Backend/database:** reuse quality-gates/scripts/logger/archive/file-generation infrastructure; exact-SHA evidence, migration/dependency/backfill/rollback review, scoped export and durable legal retention/erasure jobs. Backup manifest is not restore proof.
- **Security/integrations:** mandatory CI/protection and no bypass, isolation/no production credentials in unauthorized testing, redaction before emission, paid/provider/production authorization gates; no assertion that historical GitHub protection status is current.
- **Logging/recovery:** allowlisted operational logs separate from security and billing audit, request/trace/error/release references; bounded retry/backoff, failure alerts/incident ownership, verified restore and loss/uncertainty handling. Queue technology remains a proposal.
- **Acceptance/E2E:** exact candidate gates plus AR/EN desktop/mobile/accessibility and real customer/operator flows; refresh/history/double-click/tabs/popup-cancel/auth-expiry/offline/delayed-settlement/partial-provisioning. Guarded tests may later reuse real routes and authorized isolated fixtures; no execution in this task. Verify backup restoration, generation-safe deletion/payment races and exact release/affected smoke before readiness claim.

### First proposed implementation phase - contract/evidence foundation

**Not authorized to execute.** Static audit focuses this phase on stalled logout outcome/deadline, human-activity semantics, installed session-policy parity, account-bound workspace/revocation boundaries and existing authority reuse. It also identifies downstream legacy creation, catalog enforcement and tester/runtime seams; do not rebuild or defer these silently. After user approval only: (1) reconcile exact source audit with requirements; (2) document existing actor/session/tenant/branch/role/entitlement authority and gaps; (3) agree shared mutation/error/recovery contracts; (4) change only demonstrated gaps with compatible reuse; (5) independently verify affected boundary journeys and recovery before accepting dependent work. Proposed medium/large packages WP01A/WP01B + WP10A replace piecemeal page tasks.

Exit gate: source-backed matrix of actual callers/DB/RLS/Storage/workers/realtime/cache/export boundaries, agreed invariants and dependency ownership; no unresolved critical identity/tenant/revocation hole; approved recovery semantics; measurable denial/replay/concurrency/return acceptance. It does not require wholesale rebuilding or falsely finishing every feature first. The first read-only audit and its acceptance remain separate from later implementation tests.

### Paymob-specific proposed financial authority and acceptance

The supplied static audit confirms Paymob. The parent supplied current official research below; it informs engineering design, not new business approval or tested merchant capability. Replace generic Stripe/raw-body guidance with Paymob's documented HMAC scheme.

- Server POST processed transaction callback supplies financial authority; browser GET redirect never settles purchase. Validate documented HMAC SHA512 canonical field order and body.obj using timing-safe comparison; card-token callbacks have a separate scheme. Do not substitute a Stripe raw-body algorithm.
- Bind merchant/integration/environment/purchase/amount/currency and transaction kind/state. Authorization, capture, refund, pending and success are distinct; every successful event is not necessarily a paid purchase. Transaction-ID-linked records must apply valid later transitions, not ignore that ID forever. An order can have multiple attempts; order ID alone is not deduplication identity. Uniqueness of actual business effects prevents repeated provisioning.
- Timeout/unknown -> pending/inquiry -> same reducer, never automatic new charge. Reconcile late/double-success as financial exceptions. Paid provisioning failure stays recoverable against the original verified purchase; no automatic refund/recharge as a shortcut.
- Refunds bind original payment, total refunded bounds and concurrency; entitlement consequences require an explicit approved policy. Preserve liabilities and receipts.
- Card token alone is not a recurring mandate. MIT/subscription usage needs Moto/account capability and explicit valid consent. Select ONE approved scheduler (provider or Velora), not both. Three-day grace is a user decision; exact retry schedule is not established by examples.
- UNVERIFIED account/regional inputs: Oman activation, OMR API amount units, supported regional methods, Moto/recurring access, callback retry schedule, idempotency-key guarantees and settlement/refund timing. Preserve internal baisa accounting but do not copy EGP x100 examples to OMR. No financial effect or current account configuration checked here.
- Acceptance must prove canonical-signature fixtures for transaction and token schemes, malformed/tampered callbacks, wrong merchant/integration/currency/amount/kind, multiple attempts, pending -> success/reversal on same transaction ID, duplicate/out-of-order delivery, timeout/inquiry convergence, concurrent refund sums, late/double-success and exactly-once paid provisioning. Native/local/sandbox/real-provider evidence remain separate; no test executed by this documentation task.

Official sources supplied by parent: [Transaction callbacks](https://developers.paymob.com/paymob-docs/developers/transaction-callbacks), [HMAC](https://developers.paymob.com/paymob-docs/developers/webhook-callbacks-and-hmac/hmac), [Paymob agent integration guidance](https://developers.paymob.com/paymob-docs/ai-solutions/ai-agent-skill), [card-token HMAC](https://developers.paymob.com/paymob-docs/developers/webhook-callbacks-and-hmac/hmac/hmac-for-card-tokens), [transaction inquiry/reports](https://developers.paymob.com/paymob-docs/payments-and-features/core-features/transaction-inquiry-and-reports), [refund API](https://developers.paymob.com/paymob-docs/developers/manage-payment-apis/refund), [card token](https://developers.paymob.com/paymob-docs/developers/pay-with-saved-cards/create-card-token), [MIT](https://developers.paymob.com/paymob-docs/developers/pay-with-saved-cards/mit), [subscription plan](https://developers.paymob.com/paymob-docs/developers/subscription/create-subscription-plan).

### Engineering sources (recommendations, not product approvals)

Official research was supplied by the parent task; no new browser/network calls were made in this documentation operation. Installed framework/version compatibility and actual provider code still require source-audit reconciliation.

- [Next.js authentication](https://nextjs.org/docs/app/guides/authentication) and [Supabase server-side client](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/auth/server-side/creating-a-client?framework=nextjs&queryGroups=framework): server identity and authorization near data, Actions/API included; avoid authenticated shared-cache leakage.
- [Supabase sessions](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/auth/sessions) and [RLS](https://supabase.com/docs/guides/database/postgres/row-level-security): JWT lifetime versus sign-out, explicit strict-session checks, authoritative membership/least privilege; user_metadata and stale claims are not authorization.
- Paymob official callback/HMAC/inquiry/refund/token/MIT/subscription sources are listed in the Paymob-specific section; generic provider examples are not a substitute for those contracts.
- [Meta Embedded Signup](https://developers.facebook.com/documentation/business-messaging/whatsapp/embedded-signup/overview), [business-app users](https://developers.facebook.com/documentation/business-messaging/whatsapp/embedded-signup/onboarding-business-app-users), [Tech Provider onboarding](https://developers.facebook.com/documentation/business-messaging/whatsapp/embedded-signup/onboarding-customers-as-a-tech-provider): supplied research returned HTTP429; current exact gates UNVERIFIED. [Official Meta Postman context](https://www.postman.com/meta/whatsapp-business-platform/overview) and [Embedded Signup collection](https://www.postman.com/meta/whatsapp-business-platform/documentation/du6gzjv/embedded-signup) may include legacy details; verify at launch.
- [OWASP logging](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Logging_Cheat_Sheet.html): allowlisted records/redaction before emission and separate security/billing/operations. [AWS retries/backoff](https://aws.amazon.com/builders-library/timeouts-retries-and-backoff-with-jitter/): bounded durable retry with jitter and unknown-effect reconciliation, without mandating queue technology.
- [W3C multi-page forms](https://www.w3.org/WAI/tutorials/forms/multi-page/), [forms](https://www.w3.org/WAI/tutorials/forms/), [notifications](https://www.w3.org/WAI/tutorials/forms/notifications/): accessible steps/labels/errors and resilient recovery in the actual product journey.

## Approved commercial contract

Per branch; annual upfront = monthly x 12 x 0.8. Reply allowances remain monthly. Standard: owner + 2 employees, WhatsApp OR Instagram. Premium: owner + 4 employees, both channels. Enforcing these is CAT-002.

| Persona / tier | Monthly OMR | Annual OMR | Replies/month |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Noura Standard | 45 | 432 | 1,000 |
| Noura Premium | 90 | 864 | 3,000 |
| Lina Standard | 65 | 624 | 2,000 |
| Lina Premium | 120 | 1,152 | 6,000 |
| Dana Standard | 75 | 720 | 3,000 |
| Dana Premium | 150 | 1,440 | 10,000 |

- All: approved business FAQ/services/prices/policies in Arabic/English, enquiries/contact collection, staff handoff, history and quick replies.
- Noura Premium: case classification/tracking/routing, summaries, CSAT and alerts/reports; optional voice-message understanding remains separate.
- Lina Standard: recommendations, leads, order/quote requests and appointment details for staff confirmation. Premium: follow-up/reminders, segments/campaigns, Instagram comments/DM replies, store-bound cart recovery, sales assignments/reports, bounded workflows.
- Dana Standard: requests/bookings for staff review, status tracking, change/cancel requests, bounded reminders. Premium: operations/workflows, branch assignment, delay alerts/reports, payment follow-up with configured add-on.
- **ADD-001:** optional Premium Velora-owned website/store and cashier/POS, stock/bookings/payment/accounting, custom n8n/Make workflows. Separate selectable setup/recurring charges; scope/supplier/limits/prices not finalized.
- **VOICE-001:** actual phone conversations taking orders/bookings/appointments and voice-message handling. Supplier/number/minutes/consent contracts unfinished; no new paid trials authorized.
- **CONTENT-001:** images/reels/supported scheduled stories/content/campaigns from conversation, preview/approval and bounded publishing. Ads/WhatsApp fees separate/customer-funded; automatic WhatsApp Status publishing is not verified supported.
- Full service definitions: `src/components/app/subscription-commercial-catalog.ts`. Availability derives from proven capability, not marketing persona. Standard display retains internal legacy basic keys.

## CI Gates / main Protection / Vercel / postflight

- **CI Gates: local candidate implemented; ENFORCEMENT BLOCKED.** E-CI prepares PR/main/merge_group matrix, immutable Action SHAs, read-only permissions, locked install, offline typegen/type/lint/source tests/contracts/Build/safety and strict audit/secret baseline. Stable quality-gate rejects failed/skipped/cancelled/missing jobs. Default Vitest now selects source tests; CI excludes explicit native SQL suites. Main's last external audit had Actions/check runs = 0, only Vercel status; no workflow published or required check installed. Local checks cannot prevent a direct untested main push.
- **E-CI:** foundation b3301e5 passed locked install/full lint/typegen/tsc/49-page Build/contracts 165/165/security tests 13/13; initial 3,500-pass/2-stale-label/1-pre-existing-skip aggregate closed by Standard-label fixture 4/4. Query/multi-host scanner RED/GREEN findings closed by independent review. Follow-up 777c947 (local serial import) pins Vitest 4.1.11 + existing Vite 7.3.6 without Next/React/TS/mammoth/ESLint/Babel version changes: npm ci 460 packages passed; all 286 files/3,514 source tests passed, one pre-existing Storage ownership integration case remains skipped/required. Dynamic-import wait and explicit mock-type fixtures were corrected without weakening assertions; targeted 12/12 and 4/4, fresh typecheck, 49-page Webpack Build, scoped lint, 13 CI + 4 fixture Node tests, 1,395-path secret scan passed. Root's later strict opt-in fixture has 5 cases (18 total with CI safety); run.mjs now includes it. Static review found no material issue; no hosted/main enforcement proof.
- **GitHub main Protection: Blocked / OFF.** Branch API protected=false, protection.enabled=false, required checks enforcement=off, empty contexts. Dedicated protection read 403 (integration lacks admin access); rulesets read 403 explicitly requires GitHub Pro or public visibility. Do not make the private repo public as workaround.
- **Vercel Auto Deployment: ACTIVE, UNPROTECTED.** Main pushes actually triggered READY Production deployments. Project CLI shows Next.js/default build, buildCommand=null; its projection does not expose advanced deployment checks/full Git settings. No mandatory pre-main gate proven; successful Vercel build is not proof CI ran before main. No hosting settings changed.
- **Post-deployment Verification: scoped.** CAT/VID public checks passed; latest 6018135 deployment READY with GitHub Vercel success. It changes documentation only. Authenticated Smart/enterprise/real payment/Meta/customer AI remain unverified; no unsafe redeploy/rollback.
- **Dependency scan / SEC-001: BLOCKED.** Fresh full audit after local E-CI Vitest upgrade: 8 package entries (3 Moderate / 5 High / 0 Critical), exit 1. Earlier full 11-entry scan included 2 Critical + mocker Moderate; these are remediated locally, not on origin/main. Historical omit-dev DOCX three-entry result is narrower. Package entries are not separate CVE counts:
| Alert | Locked package / dependency | Advisory / affected execution |

- **Prepared locally, not externally applied:** E-CI required contexts above. Affected high-risk changes separately need isolated native Auth/PostgREST/RLS acceptance; never Production customer fixtures/credentials/paid providers.
- **Enforce before main:** suitable private-repo protection entitlement, mandatory PR checks and current base/merge queue, no direct-main/admin/actor bypass. Running checks only after push main is insufficient because Vercel can start concurrently. Existing scripts/integration/vitest.enterprise.config.mts and offline guards are candidates to adapt, not proven mandatory CI.
- **Practical proof UNRUN:** failing PR cannot merge/reach main; passing PR runs every required job, integrates serially on latest main, deploys that exact SHA READY, and passes affected smoke. Hold main publication until this is proven or requirement explicitly revised; do not claim protected automatic deployment ready.
- EXEC-001 authorizes local workflow/gate preparation alongside registration implementation/tests. External protection and hosting/secret/Production changes are not applied here; sensitive permission/plan/secret changes retain separate authorization. Required checks must be proven before main publication; no passwords/keys requested.

## Dependency Map / critical path

Execution priority: `FLOW-001 STAGE-002 -> 003 -> 004/005 -> applicable 006 -> 007 -> 008/009 -> 010/011 -> 012 -> 013/014`; R/Flow tables give exact acceptance. OPS-001 and recovery are parallel release gates, not permission to skip early user stages or a requirement to defer safe local verification.

- OPS-001 blocks main delivery, not authorized isolated coding. Identity/session/company/branch contracts are shared prerequisites for every mutation/order/worker/sender.
- ONB/review/knowledge prepare a business independently; payment/review/native connection/actual AI readiness converge before activation. Deferred SMS is not authorization to bypass confirmation.
- CAT-002 needs branch scope/immutable finance/compatible SQL; annual paid duration must coexist with monthly usage. Legacy Master 500/1500 credit/upgrade assumptions must not override approved 1000/3000 Noura quotas.
- Enterprise foundation is integrated; branch assignment/routing/final dispatch remains critical for multiple-branch activation. UI filtering alone cannot close it.
- Add-on voice/media/site/POS/accounting/workflows need supplier/cost/consent/capability and core paid/channel contracts, not generic flags.
- **Recovery blocker:** docs/recovery/README.md records an encrypted scoped snapshot with authenticated decryption/hash/count verification, not full Supabase Auth/Storage backup or tested SQL restore. Full recovery proof precedes sensitive Production changes; keep key outside repository.

QA acceptance inventory (TASK-QA-CENTER-001; all queued, not implemented):
- **QA-JOURNEY:** actual account registration/verification/login -> blank local form/suggestions/branches/packages/one term -> checkout/sandbox settlement -> paid provisioning -> settings/staff/knowledge/channels/AI trial/operation -> renewal/cancellation. Overview reads actual scenario counts/status/last-run/environment/release/error references, never constants.
- **QA-PAYMENT:** successful/failed/pending/unknown payment; duplicate/delayed/replayed webhook; double checkout; browser closed or local data lost after payment; provisioning failure and retry; reversal; foreign order denial. Each proves actual state and a receipt.
- **QA-SUBSCRIPTION:** monthly/annual/multi-branch/mixed packages; unified dates; once-only first bonus; seven-day deadline/provider delay/shared outage; later branch; quota expiry/monthly reset; selective renewal/mandate/failure/three-day grace/cancellation/reactivation/monthly-to-annual. Time controls cover +1d/+7d/month/year/grace/renewal/expiry through injected clock only.
- **QA-AUTHORIZATION:** user A/B company denial; unauthorized staff branch; revoked staff old session; forged company/branch/role/amount/slot count; foreign paid order; stale previous-account browser state; cross-tenant provider credentials. Passed means the attempted unauthorized operation was denied by the actual server.
- **QA-CHANNEL-AI:** WA connection/Instagram authorization; missing permission/expired token/wrong asset; webhook/send failure; duplicate inbound; human handoff; AI without entitlement/approved knowledge/quota; branch routing; reconnect/resume. Adapter/sandbox success cannot close real-provider launch gates.

### Final Design Brief02:00 - lifecycle access boundaries and owner Business information

Source USER Sentinel_b0c13d6b3ea08191985c6b463b5f19a5,2026-10-16 02:00UTC. Accepted: bounded code-audit findings and light/white/teal IBM Plex style; explicitly NOT acceptance of current navigation as final design. Scope is documentation-only final brief for review; no code, tests, new image, paid operation or production authorization. This section supersedes earlier language implying the current six links/five tabs must be final navigation. Homepage and all inherited/shared styling impacts remain excluded.

#### Lifecycle/access matrix - TARGET contract, current gaps kept explicit

Authentication/account recovery, owned financial evidence/reconciliation and permitted support must be reachable independently of active-service status. Role/tenant/branch authority is checked separately on every read, action and RPC. Payment settlement, paid ownership, provisioning, configuration and operational readiness are distinct facts; a company row is not payment proof.

| Lifecycle / actor | Target reachable context and rights | Target limits / transition evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Visitor | Public discovery/legal and sign-in/account creation; homepage unchanged | No tenant data or operational dashboard |
| Authenticated / verification or profile incomplete | Own account, verification/profile completion, safe return and owned recovery/support | No service entitlement inferred from login; verify intended user/session |
| Prepayment local preparation | Separate preparation/review journey, account return, approved local inputs and commercial comparison | Memory-only business/branch preparation; no paid company provisioning, live integrations or full operational sidebar |
| Financial review before collection | Explicit immutable package/term/currency/amount/scope review and deliberate payment handoff | Review/offer is not settlement; maintain original attempt identity and consent |
| Payment pending or UNKNOWN | Owned order status, authoritative reconciliation, account/support, retained local preparation | No fresh payment/recharge on UNKNOWN; no fake success, activation or paid workspace creation |
| Settled payment, company/branch absent or partially provisioned | Paid-purchase recovery and existing order evidence, idempotent original provisioning repair | No second purchase; settlement receipt must bind actor, offer and intended provision scope; no company existence shortcut |
| Settled and provisioned, initial channel/actual AI unready | Paid configuration/setup: owned business settings, permitted people/knowledge/review/channel connection, billing/status/support | These paid setup rights do NOT require operational readiness. Show concrete blockers/recovery. Initial paid clock starts only under approved same-scope channel+actual-AI readiness rules; preparation delay must not consume paid time |
| Active operational service | Operational workspace for allowed channels, inbox/AI/services/usage and authorized management | Action/RPC/final-dispatch authority, scope, quota and actual readiness remain required; visible menu is not permission |
| Approved three-day renewal grace | Honest grace status, payment/renewal/recovery/support and only operations explicitly allowed by lifecycle policy | Grace is separate from initial activation wait; exact per-action grace capabilities need contract confirmation, never silently grant all operations |
| Expired or service suspended | Account, owned invoices/orders/renewal or eligible reactivation, support and permitted retained-data access | Block service execution according to authoritative reason. Reauthentication/security/tenant suspension can independently limit access; do not erase financial rights or promise operational recovery merely from UI |
| Archived / exit processing | Retained owned finance, support and lawful export/deletion/retention-request paths under approved policy | Archive request, scheduled exit, completed archive and legal retention distinct; no blanket deletion/reactivation promise |
| Invited employee / manager | Correct-account invitation acceptance/recovery, explicit company/branch/capability/seat scope; then appropriate setup/operational views | Invitation/manager title not owner privilege or paid proof. No owner-only company-profile editing by assumption; delegation ceiling and current grants must authorize each action |

CURRENT evidence and gap: src/app/app/layout.tsx:7 AppLayout checks authentication/verification/profile/company/context, not a paid entitlement or operational-readiness condition. src/app/setup/page.tsx:22 chooses PrepaymentPreparation when company absent and SetupWizard when present. Thus company existence currently permits workspace entry without proved paid entitlement at this boundary; this is a static design/authorization gap, not a demonstrated deployed exploit. Downstream actions/RPCs have independent checks; this finding does not establish all backend operations are exposed. Installed database, middleware composition and runtime remain unverified.

Fix contract belongs to PF01 WP01A/01B (session/scope/classified access decisions), PF03 WP03A (settlement authority) +WP03B (paid provisioning/recovery), PF04 WP04A/B (paid configuration), PF05 WP05A (actual channel state), PF07 WP07A (initial readiness/clock vs renewal/grace) +WP07B (expiry/exit/retained finance). Do not add one blanket active-subscription redirect to the entire /account, /setup or /app tree. Separate route eligibility from action capability and final dispatch checks. Legacy/missing entitlement/schema evidence needs explicit non-destructive compatibility/recovery handling, not silent paid proof or blanket lockout.

#### Proposed navigation hierarchy - review proposal, separate from source inventory

Prepayment: concise journey navigation for account -> local preparation -> financial review -> payment status/recovery, with persistent account/support/legal exits. No full customer operational sidebar or unsupported live controls. Paid-unready setup: focused checklist/task navigation to owned setup areas, channel/AI readiness evidence and financial recovery; do not block those tasks until service activation. Active workspace: propose role-filtered groups Workspace (overview/inbox and actually available entitled services), Business management (business profile, branch/people scope, knowledge/assistant, channel management), Subscription (plan/usage, orders/renewal/compensation where authorized), and Account/help. These are conceptual groups, NOT approved labels/routes or live new screens. Existing routes/children may compose them after approval; unsupported capabilities remain identified as planned/unavailable, never fake working tools.

Keep every approved capability discoverable through role/capability-filtered destinations and existing J01-J36/CF/UXC mappings. Do not remove assistant test, Instagram connection, team, branches, activation, usage or financial recovery merely because the first picture contains four fields. Managers/employees see only granted branch/service functions; owner-only company profile remains owner-only. Platform administration retains its separate protected navigation. Final group names/order, settings-tab vs grouped-page treatment and mobile composition require USER design choice. Current six sidebar links and five settings tabs are evidence/reference only, not mandatory final IA.

#### Exact screen specification and source contracts

Screen scope is owner business-profile editing for selected existing paid company in the TARGET management journey; source route /app/settings?tab=business, J06/J15 and applicable S01-S12/C09-C15, WP01A/04A. BusinessTab in src/components/app/settings.tsx:218-285 has exactly four editable fields in source order: اسم النشاط / Business name (required text), المدينة / City (optional text), أوقات العمل / Working hours (optional free text), رقم للتواصل معك / A phone number to reach you (optional text with inputMode tel and LTR). No business email, country/type/reply-language editor, branches tab, weekly schedule or ordinary discard button. Account identity summary may appear OUTSIDE the form as explicitly read-only context; account email must not resemble a fifth business input. Save is حفظ / Save; busy جارٍ الحفظ… / Saving…. Conditional conflict action is استخدام أحدث القيم المتعارضة / Use latest conflicting values, not ordinary discard.

Current visual evidence: WorkspaceShell sets RTL; internal-ui.module.css:32-60 puts a232px sidebar on the right in Arabic desktop, switches <=1023px to top header/collapsed menu; ui.tsx:341 uses all five owner tabs in a horizontal scroller. Proposed style can reshape this navigation only after USER review; retain full role capability discoverability. Phone must not obscure fields/errors with save or keyboard. Proposed label rename معرفة المساعد for /app/knowledge and بيانات النشاط for business tab is STILL pending; v2 retains current labels.

Handler/settings.tsx:250 -> tenancy/actions.ts:915 saveOwnerBusiness(expected user,company,version; name/city/hours/phone plus preserved hidden businessType/replyLanguage/country/contactEmail) -> versioned update_owner_company_profile -> public.companies display_name,city,hours,contact_phone plus business_type,reply_language,contact_email,updated_at/version; audit private.company_profile_write_audit. The eight-key SQL patch retains country; attempted difference creates private.company_sensitive_change_requests and REVIEW_REQUIRED without profile change. Native-session/verified active owner/non-suspended company/version checks are explicit in 20261014015934_master_business_authority.sql:93-116. Hidden preserved fields are backend payload, not permission to add UI inputs. Legacy upsert fallback is distinct and migration/deployment compatibility remains unverified. No new backend implementation is implied.

#### Target acceptance and approval gates - NOT RUN

1. Visitor/prepayment never acquires tenant service by UI navigation; settlement UNKNOWN retains same order and recovery without new collection; settled-without-company recovery provisions original scope once.
2. Paid but unready owner can complete authorized setup and access finance/support without fake active status or early clock; initial wait, normal renewal and grace obey separate approved contracts.
3. Expired/suspended/archived service restrictions preserve appropriately authorized account/financial/support rights; wrong tenant/branch/session and revoked invitations fail closed at action/RPC, not just menu.
4. Owner sees four fields only; employee/manager cannot write owner profile by changing URL/payload; hidden values preserved; phone/name validation, stale version, conflict, UNKNOWN and committed-refresh-failure distinct. No automatic replay after unknown result.
5. AR RTL/EN LTR and phone navigation expose all approved role capabilities without pretending unavailable functions work; keyboard/focus/errors/zoom and layout need later authorized runtime acceptance. Homepage isolation must include inherited styles/fonts/portals/primitives.
