### Consolidated Design Brief02:05 - separate account, context, access recovery and business profile

Sources USER Sentinel_5786614cdbe88191b7690d987b51e63c and Sentinel_e76cc6ca5fa48191b5e83269a5fb26a4. Deliver full brief text in main conversation before another image. Only general light/white/teal IBM Plex style accepted. Page/layout/navigation NOT approved for code. v2 remains historical visual reference, its mixed identity/access/business presentation superseded by this proposed separation. No new pages/fields, code/tests/image or production activity. Homepage/inherited fonts/themes/primitives excluded.

Current proof: settings.tsx:85-96 puts signed-account email/company/role, conditional context selector, platform link and Refresh access above all settings tabs. BusinessTab:218-285 is owner-only and renders four inputs. AccountTab calls AccountSettingsLinkCard (account-page.tsx:223) linking EXISTING /account, also accessible via AccountMenu (account-chrome.tsx:356). src/app/account/page.tsx uses getAccountProfile, verified email/profile completion and AccountPage; no active-service prerequisite in this page. AccountPage:119-208 edits personal name via updateAccountName, email is read-only, password recovery links /forgot-password. masterJourneyEnabled conditionally exposes /verify-phone, /account/recovery, business-management/create and scoped operator links; do not show them unconditionally or invent inline phone/password/email editing. Copy-email/support-code actions and TeamSelfPanel already exist and must remain discoverable in their authorized account context.

Immediate personal-name contract: tenancy/actions.ts:627 updateAccountName(name,expectedUserId) -> readSessionIdentity, getUser, verified contact, trim2-120 -> profiles upsert user_id/full_name, read support_code. Optional active company_members.member_name sync and revalidation do not undo committed name write. This is personal identity, not companies.contact_email/contact_phone. AccountSessionProvider.refresh (account-chrome.tsx:90) calls getAccountProfile with request-generation fencing, classifies failures and reloads on identity/company change; it is not an entitlement grant or a backend rights-repair command. isCurrentUser alone is not native-session/operation fencing. Existing settings Refresh access fires identity refresh and router.refresh without an explicit unified outcome receipt; contextual outcome/recovery presentation needs implementation later, not just moving its button.

Classification: (CURRENT PROOF) separate /account and owner BusinessTab contracts already exist. (PRESENTATION PROPOSAL) use /account for personal identity/contact/security and its existing entry, reduce company/role to compact shell metadata, place access refresh in clearly discoverable contextual recovery/menu rather than dominant normal-state form card. No new page is needed. Do not hide security context or permanently remove Refresh access: keep named access through account/context menu and expose beside stale/denied/unavailable messages. (FUNCTIONAL GAP) precise recovery result, stale-session write fencing, payment-vs-company gating and scoped branch-grant interface remain contract/implementation work; repositioning card alone cannot fix them. (UNRESOLVED POLICY) employee/manager company-profile read permission, exact grace/retained-data capabilities and final nav/rename require separate decisions.

| Actual actor/state | Navigation/context | Company profile visible/editable | Personal account | Branch grant / other gates |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Owner, selected company | Current six owner links/five settings tabs are evidence, not final IA; compact company+owner context proposed | Current owner form visible/editable four fields; server owner/version/session checks independent | Existing /account own name editable, email read-only; conditional phone/recovery/security links | Branch grants/setup belong separate authorized family, not new profile controls; paid setup distinct from operational readiness |
| Employee (staff) | Current Conversations/Settings plus Business info if knowledge.read; account tab only | Current BusinessTab not rendered, requested owner tab locked; no assumed read-only permission | Own /account identity contract, not owner privileges | Branch/company capability required downstream; no owner profile edit |
| Branch manager | No manager role branch in settings/shell; if represented as staff it receives that UI subset, otherwise actual context must establish role | No owner override and no implied companywide read/write; a future read-only view requires explicit scoped reader/response projection | Own account, same identity boundary | Existing enterprise grants are not proof of a complete branch-grant editor; targeted supplied mapping retains UI gap |
| No selected context | Choose-company metadata/selector and recovery, not tenant form | effectiveRole null; form not rendered | Own account still independently available when identity authorized | Never infer target from old company/draft; selection is company, not assumed branch hierarchy |
| Denied/unavailable/expired session | Explicit reason and safe retry/account/support or sign-in action; no stale tenant values | No disabled unauthorized populated form | Only actions allowed by actual identity/security state | Refresh cannot self-grant rights; service entitlement separate from authentication |

Important data boundary: page.tsx:17-43 currently passes initialCompany from getCompanyAccess as a client prop without owner-specific projection, although UI hides BusinessTab for staff. Do not claim the source proves owner-only data delivery or a dedicated authorized employee reader. Review source reader/projection before proposing employee read-only company profile; this bounded finding alone is not a deployed data-leak/exploit claim. Hidden businessType/replyLanguage/country/contactEmail are preserved owner write payload, not four additional visible fields or authorization evidence.

DESKTOP BRIEF, proposed next image: existing /app/settings?tab=business, owner, selected existing company, intended paid management scenario without invented settled/active badge. Arabic RTL1440x1000, separately English LTR later; white surfaces/quiet teal/IBM Plex. Compact shell context shows company and role, conditional existing company selection, language and account-menu access to /account. Branch context appears only when authoritative branch contract supplies it; never fabricate selector, grants or branch count. Main title uses existing Business information / معلومات النشاط pending proposed rename. Main content is one clear business form, no large account-email/role/access-refresh card. A small visible account link remains in menu/context; identity information is not an editable business field. Access-refresh remains discoverable through named context/recovery action, not silently deleted. Normal state need not show a warning.

Four fields only, same source order: اسم النشاط / Business name required plain text, المدينة / City optional plain text, أوقات العمل / Working hours optional free text, رقم للتواصل معك / A phone number to reach you optional text with LTR/inputMode tel. Name server trim/nonblank/max120; optional phone blank or normalized +7-15digits. No invented placeholders implying verified number, structured schedule or live integration. No business email/country/type/reply-language/branches input, no ordinary discard button. One submit حفظ / Save, busy جارٍ الحفظ… / Saving…; conditional استخدام أحدث القيم المتعارضة / Use latest conflicting values only when actual conflicting fields exist. Account form has its own Cancel; never transplant it into business form.

NAVIGATION proposal, not final approval: visitor/auth/local-prepayment journey uses compact account/preparation/financial review/order-status-recovery navigation. Do not show active inbox/channels dashboard as if paid. Pending/UNKNOWN order keeps original attempt, status/recovery/support and no fresh collection. Settled-no-company goes paid provisioning recovery, not buy again. Paid-provisioned but unready can access authorized business/people/knowledge/channel setup and finances/support; operational readiness must not block paid setup or start clock early. Operational workspace role-filtered conceptual groups: Workspace, Business management, Subscription, Account/help; all existing approved capabilities remain discoverable via existing routes/children and J36/UXC mappings. Current six links and five tabs can be reorganized ONLY as proposal with user review. Expired/grace/suspended/archived retain authorized identity/financial/support rights; action capabilities, not a blanket active-service gate. Owner/manager/employee views differ by real grants, not design assumption. Platform admin separate. Proposed معرفة المساعد (/app/knowledge) and بيانات النشاط (business tab) still not approved.

STATE COPY below is proposed semantic copy, not a claim current UI already implements differentiated states. Unchanged: disabled Save, no success message. Dirty: Save enabled if name present/no conflict; retain input. Saving: جارٍ الحفظ… / Saving…, disable duplicate submit. Valid confirmed save: تم حفظ معلومات النشاط / Business information saved. Validation: point to actual invalid field; name missing/too long or invalid international phone, preserve input. Permission: لا تملك صلاحية تعديل معلومات هذا النشاط / You do not have permission to edit this business profile; no data-bearing form for unauthorized reader. Session loss: انتهت الجلسة. سجّل الدخول للمتابعة / Session expired. Sign in to continue; quarantine captured session. Conflict: تغيّرت بعض المعلومات المحفوظة. راجع القيم الأحدث قبل الحفظ / Saved information changed. Review the latest values before saving; use existing adopt-conflicting-values action, no silent overwrite. UNKNOWN: تعذّر تأكيد نتيجة الحفظ. تحقّق من البيانات المحفوظة قبل المحاولة مجددًا / Could not confirm the save result. Check saved information before trying again; no automatic retry or success/failure fiction. Confirmed save + refresh failure: تم الحفظ، لكن تعذّر تحديث العرض / Saved, but the view could not refresh; retry read, not write. Recovery button copy should describe refreshing access/data without promising grant changes. Current failed business saves use generic authFailed; structured field/outcome copy requires later contract work.

MOBILE BRIEF: Arabic RTL390x844, compact header/menu/context, one-column four-field form, account entry discoverable in menu, no dominant identity card. Preserve all allowed navigation; horizontal tabs may remain if chosen, with overflow cue, or a clearly proposed accessible selector preserving destinations. No vanished settings/financial/security actions. Phone value remains LTR; English mirrors layout/order direction while preserving semantic sequence. Save must not cover field/error/keyboard; prefer in-flow action for first proposal. No icon-only unlabeled recovery/menu/account action. Required/optional explicit, associated labels, keyboard/focus/zoom/error description and text status with non-color cues; >=44px control targets proposed. Images cannot prove WCAG/keyboard/runtime correctness.

CONTRACT/ACCEPTANCE: reuse WorkspaceShell/AccountMenu/AccountSettingsLinkCard/AccountPage/BusinessTab/Field/Button and existing route boundaries; presentation extraction must not change server business authority, field ownership, checkout/activation clocks or homepage inheritance. Save contract and columns remain the preceding02:00 brief: saveOwnerBusiness -> update_owner_company_profile -> companies + audit/version; native-session/owner/version checks, hidden values preserved, country change review separate. Applicable J06/J15/S01-S12/C09-C15 WP01A/04A, account J02-J06/WP01A/02A, lifecycle WP03A/B/05A/07A/B. Target acceptance NOT RUN: owner four inputs; staff/manager denied profile and scoped navigation; account own edits independent; no unauthorized data in disabled fields/props; context change cannot target old company/session; validation/conflict/UNKNOWN/committed-refresh distinctly recover; every existing authorized account/context/recovery action discoverable desktop/mobile; homepage untouched.

NEXT GATES: parent/user review this complete brief first; then next desktop/mobile image, explicit USER screen/state review and separate implementation permission. No page is ready for programming. Need user choices on final IA/tab grouping/mobile treatment and optional rename; need policy/reader contract before any employee/manager read-only company profile. v2 mixed-card layout superseded as presentation proposal, style retained. No new image generated by writer.
### Meta foundational acceptance02:19 - official evidence, source contracts and staged repair

Authorization/source USER Sentinel_e16b8ce352ac8191b2eaa6de396d4415,2026-10-16 02:19UTC. Integrated supplied WA/IG official-source research checked2026-10-16 and independent bounded six-family source audit. Writer did not execute browsers/tests/provider/config/DB operations or inspect secrets. No Astro/Astra/model delegation. Source definitions are not deployed schema, installed permission or actual dashboard proof. Meta acceptance is an ongoing PF05/PF06/PF10 foundation, not a final checkbox. Existing homepage freeze and MAINCHAT USER image approval plus separate code approval remain. General style approval is not Meta readiness or new-page authorization.

#### A. Actual reviewer journey: documented requirements versus proposed demonstration

Meta reference app Going to production lists real privacy policy, Tech Provider Configuration ID, business verification, App Review, publication, webhooks and valid OAuth redirects: https://github.com/fbsamples/business-messaging-sample-tech-provider-app#going-to-production . Its checklist is reference guidance, not proof Velora passed any gate. The source and Meta Cloud API collection support whatsapp_business_management + whatsapp_business_messaging as basic WA permissions: https://www.postman.com/meta/whatsapp-business-platform/collection/wlk6lh4/whatsapp-cloud-api . business_management is use-case dependent (e.g. portfolio queries), not a reason to request it automatically. Development access is restricted to app-role users. Business verification, Tech Provider onboarding, access verification, publication, App Review and individual Advanced Access are separate evidence requirements, not interchangeable green states.

IG Login supports Business/Creator without a required linked Facebook Page; instagram_business_basic/manage_messages/manage_comments/content_publish are the current scope family described by Meta-owned collection; legacy business_* deprecated Jan27 2025: https://www.postman.com/meta/workspace/instagram/documentation/23987686-9386f468-7714-490f-9bfc-9442db5c8f00 and https://www.postman.com/meta/instagram/documentation/6yqw8pt/instagram-api?entity=request-23987686-3a63181c-e5c6-4890-96dd-e4d425bef5e0 . Basic+manage_messages is candidate least privilege for implemented messaging. Included Lina comments remain REQUIRED product scope: stage manage_comments and actual comments proof; do not silently drop that promise because current code subscribes messages only. Publishing permission only for a separately approved implemented use case. Different Facebook Login path requires linked Page; do not mix its Page tokens/scopes/config with IG Login: https://www.postman.com/meta/instagram/documentation/6yqw8pt/instagram-api?entity=request-23987686-ab559ffb-8e2c-4b0a-b43a-5737b6d2f672 . FB-path conversations reference uses Page MESSAGING task/instagram_basic/instagram_manage_messages/pages_manage_metadata and discusses Advanced Access/non-role/verified-business context, not universal IG Login dashboard requirements: https://www.postman.com/meta/messenger-platform-api/folder/22794852-255610cd-47f5-4f4d-b3fa-71aec360be9a . Do not add pages_messaging merely because this is a Messenger collection.

PROPOSED reviewer sequence on real Velora pages: legitimate reviewer identity/access -> feature purpose -> correct channel OAuth consent -> selected eligible asset -> per-permission real feature result -> signed inbound appearance -> authorized reply/provider acceptance receipt -> reconnect/failure handling -> accurate privacy/deletion pathway. Map feature -> permission -> endpoint -> data -> reviewer proof -> retention/deletion. Suggested video records that sequence without secrets, fake success or a duplicate review site. Exact video language/length/format, test-call counts, test-account delivery mechanism and current App Review prompts are NOT verified; retrieve actual dashboard requirements before submission. Reviewer access must work without an unexpected customer checkout barrier but must use lawful bounded authority, never a bypass of paid customer protection.

Velora internal review grants are NOT Meta requirements: src/lib/meta-review/actions.ts and 20261013162534_meta_review_manual_authority.sql bind platform-admin grants to active company/owner/channel/app/asset/source account/signing fingerprint,1-5 recipients,7days/50sends and intent/claim receipt. WA additionally requires configured meta-test company/phone/WABA; IG its review company. Private RLS/no-direct-access authority is source evidence only. Grant/use is NOT general customer entitlement, paid activation, actual AI proof or approval to contact users now.

#### B. Real customer journey and approved policy

Discovery/signup/verified identity -> truthful offer selection/local prepayment preparation -> immutable financial review -> original payment attempt -> authoritative settlement -> idempotent paid company/branch provisioning/recovery -> paid setup -> explicit scoped Meta authorization -> verified assets/subscription/number or professional-account state -> real signed inbound and accepted reply -> separately proved same-scope AI/config/review readiness -> operational service under final-dispatch rights/quota. Pending/UNKNOWN payment retains original attempt and recovery; no new collection. Paid but unready retains setup/finance/support rights. Initial paid clock requires SAME scope channel AND actual AI ready; seven-day lateness is explicit and delay does not consume paid time. Review manual sends do not satisfy that barrier. Normal renewal/grace remain separate approved lifecycle rules.

Paid-first is Velora policy; no Meta mandate found. Reviewer journey is not the customer purchase journey. Choose new Cloud number, existing API path and eligible Business App Coexistence separately; no automatic deregistration/deletion/migration or invented eligibility rule. Customer branch purchase-to-provider asset allocation is not established by company/link/generation contracts and requires a scoped contract. Employees/managers need authorized scopes, not personal App Review; owner profile rights remain separate.
